#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
        
        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
            
            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }
        
        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
    printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
    printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Initialize bodies
    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);
    randomizeBodies(bodies);
    
    // Run simulation
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies);
        integrateBodies(bodies);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }
    
    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
        
        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

--- hash ---
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

codex
The benchmark is a straightforward all-pairs O(N²) solver. I’m using a 1D contiguous decomposition: each MPI rank owns a body range, all ranks hold the current global body state, and an `MPI_Allgatherv` exchanges only the updated owned ranges after each timestep. This keeps each target’s source-body summation order identical to the original, which preserves deterministic results and the existing hash format while avoiding redundant force calculations.

+        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);

+            ? 0
+            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];

#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include <mpi.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
constexpr double DT = 0.01;

struct Vec3 {
    double x, y, z;
    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
};

struct Body {
    Vec3 pos;
    Vec3 vel;
};

static_assert(sizeof(Vec3) == 3 * sizeof(double));
static_assert(sizeof(Body) == 6 * sizeof(double));

void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    for (auto& body : bodies) {
        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    }
}

void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const size_t firstBody, const size_t lastBody) {
    const size_t n = bodies.size();

    for (size_t i = firstBody; i < lastBody; ++i) {
        const double posX = bodies[i].pos.x;
        const double posY = bodies[i].pos.y;
        const double posZ = bodies[i].pos.z;
        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;

        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - posX;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - posY;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - posZ;
            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;

            Fx += dx * invDist3;
            Fy += dy * invDist3;
            Fz += dz * invDist3;
        }

        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    }
}

void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const size_t firstBody, const size_t lastBody) {
    for (size_t i = firstBody; i < lastBody; ++i) {
        auto& body = bodies[i];
        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    }
}

double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    double energy = 0.0;
    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    
    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    }
    
    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
        }
    }
    
    return energy;
}

// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
        // Check for NaN or Inf values
        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
            return false;
        }
        
        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
        const double maxPos = 1e6;
        const double maxVel = 1e6;
        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    int rank = 0;
    int worldSize = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);

    int numBodies = 1024;
    int numSteps = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    if (numBodies < 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            printf("Number of bodies must be non-negative\n");
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return 1;
    }

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    }

    // Bodies are distributed in contiguous ranges.  Every rank keeps the
    // complete source state because each force calculation needs every body.
    std::vector<int> bodyCounts(worldSize);
    std::vector<int> bodyDisplacements(worldSize);
    const int bodiesPerRank = numBodies / worldSize;
    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
        bodyDisplacements[process] = process == 0
            ? 0
            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];
    }

    std::vector<Body> bodies(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
    if (rank == 0) {
        randomizeBodies(bodies);
    }

    // Describe the two Vec3 members without assuming that Body is an MPI
    // primitive type.  Its extent is exactly sizeof(Body), so counts and
    // displacements below are expressed in bodies rather than bytes.
    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
    int blockLengths[2] = {3, 3};
    MPI_Aint memberDisplacements[2] = {
        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos)),
        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, vel))
    };
    MPI_Datatype memberTypes[2] = {MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_DOUBLE};
    MPI_Type_create_struct(2, blockLengths, memberDisplacements, memberTypes, &mpiBody);
    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);

    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);

    // Scatter the initial state, then make it available to every rank.  The
    // root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also its receive
    // buffer for the root-owned range.
    if (numBodies > 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        } else {
            MPI_Scatterv(nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, mpiBody,
                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
        integrateBodies(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);

        // The next timestep needs the newly integrated positions and
        // velocities from every rank.  In-place allgather avoids an extra
        // local-to-global copy on the critical path.
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double elapsed = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }

    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    int exitCode = 0;
    if (validate && rank == 0) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");

        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            exitCode = 1;
        }
    }

    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBody);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return exitCode;
}

+        if (rank == 0) {
+            printf("Number of bodies must be non-negative\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
+        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
+        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
+        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+    }
+
+    // Bodies are distributed in contiguous ranges.  Every rank keeps the
+    // complete source state because each force calculation needs every body.
+    std::vector<int> bodyCounts(worldSize);
+    std::vector<int> bodyDisplacements(worldSize);
+    const int bodiesPerRank = numBodies / worldSize;
+    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
+    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        bodyDisplacements[process] = process == 0
+            ? 0
+            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];

+    std::vector<Body> bodies(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+    if (rank == 0) {

+    // Describe the two Vec3 members without assuming that Body is an MPI
+    // primitive type.  Its extent is exactly sizeof(Body), so counts and
+    // displacements below are expressed in bodies rather than bytes.
+    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
+    int blockLengths[2] = {3, 3};
+    MPI_Aint memberDisplacements[2] = {
+        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos)),
+        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, vel))

+    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);
+
+    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];

+

+    // root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also its receive

-    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-

-    if (printResults) {

+        if (rank == 0) {
+            printf("Number of bodies must be non-negative\n");
+        }
+        MPI_Finalize();
+        return 1;
+    }
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
+        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
+        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
+        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+    }
+
+    // Bodies are distributed in contiguous ranges.  Every rank keeps the
+    // complete source state because each force calculation needs every body.
+    std::vector<int> bodyCounts(worldSize);
+    std::vector<int> bodyDisplacements(worldSize);
+    const int bodiesPerRank = numBodies / worldSize;
+    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
+    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
+        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        bodyDisplacements[process] = process == 0
+            ? 0
+            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<Body> bodies(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        randomizeBodies(bodies);
+    }
+
+    // Describe the two Vec3 members without assuming that Body is an MPI
+    // primitive type.  Its extent is exactly sizeof(Body), so counts and
+    // displacements below are expressed in bodies rather than bytes.
+    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
+    int blockLengths[2] = {3, 3};
+    MPI_Aint memberDisplacements[2] = {
+        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos)),
+        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, vel))
+    };
+    MPI_Datatype memberTypes[2] = {MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_DOUBLE};
+    MPI_Type_create_struct(2, blockLengths, memberDisplacements, memberTypes, &mpiBody);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);
+
+    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
+    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
+    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);
+
+    // Scatter the initial state, then make it available to every rank.  The
+    // root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also its receive
+    // buffer for the root-owned range.
+    if (numBodies > 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Scatterv(nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, mpiBody,
+                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
+                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

-        computeForces(bodies);
-        integrateBodies(bodies);
+        computeForces(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
+        integrateBodies(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
+
+        // The next timestep needs the newly integrated positions and
+        // velocities from every rank.  In-place allgather avoids an extra
+        // local-to-global copy on the critical path.
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
+                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);

codex
The larger run confirms identical hashes across 1 and 4 ranks. I found one further safe optimization for the distributed path: force evaluation depends only on positions, so timestep exchanges can use a resized MPI datatype containing just the three position values. Full body records are gathered once at the end only when `-r` or `-v` requires velocities on rank 0. This cuts per-step communication roughly in half without changing numerical operations or output.
apply patch

+            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];

    // to Body so body counts/displacements remain directly usable while each
    // timestep exchanges only the position member.
    MPI_Datatype mpiPositionMember;
    MPI_Datatype mpiBodyPositions;
    int positionBlockLength = 3;
    MPI_Aint positionDisplacement = static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos));
    MPI_Type_create_struct(1, &positionBlockLength, &positionDisplacement,
                           memberTypes, &mpiPositionMember);
    MPI_Type_create_resized(mpiPositionMember, 0, static_cast<MPI_Aint>(sizeof(Body)),
                            &mpiBodyPositions);
    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBodyPositions);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiPositionMember);

    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);

    // Scatter the initial state, then make all positions available to every
    // rank.  The root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also
    // its receive buffer for the root-owned range.
    if (numBodies > 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }

                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
        integrateBodies(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);

        // The next timestep needs the newly integrated positions and
        // velocities from every rank.  In-place allgather avoids an extra
        // local-to-global copy on the critical path.
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double elapsed = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
    }

    // Velocities are private to their owning rank during the simulation.  A
    // full-state gather is needed only for rank 0's optional result output or
    // validation, and is deliberately outside the timed region.
    if ((printResults || validate) && numBodies > 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
                        bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        } else {
            MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody,
                        nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }

    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    int exitCode = 0;
    if (validate && rank == 0) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");

        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            exitCode = 1;
        }
    }

    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBodyPositions);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBody);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return exitCode;
}

+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,

+    if (rank == 0) {

codex
The position-only exchange is verified: single-rank and multi-rank hashes remain identical, including uneven partitions and ranks with zero owned bodies. Full-state gathering is also tested for both validation and result printing, while the no-output path skips that gather as intended. I’m completing the final build/status audit now.

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)

# Enable optimizations

# MPI is required: the executable always uses distributed-memory parallelism.
target_link_libraries(nbody PRIVATE MPI::MPI_CXX m)
        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");

    // Bodies are distributed in contiguous ranges.  Every rank keeps the
    // complete source state because each force calculation needs every body.
    std::vector<int> bodyCounts(worldSize);
    std::vector<int> bodyDisplacements(worldSize);
    const int bodiesPerRank = numBodies / worldSize;
    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
        bodyDisplacements[process] = process == 0
            ? 0
            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];
    }

    std::vector<Body> bodies(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
    if (rank == 0) {
        randomizeBodies(bodies);
    }

    // Describe the two Vec3 members without assuming that Body is an MPI
    // primitive type.  Its extent is exactly sizeof(Body), so counts and
    // displacements below are expressed in bodies rather than bytes.
    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
    int blockLengths[2] = {3, 3};
    MPI_Aint memberDisplacements[2] = {
        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos)),
        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, vel))
    };
    MPI_Datatype memberTypes[2] = {MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_DOUBLE};
    MPI_Type_create_struct(2, blockLengths, memberDisplacements, memberTypes, &mpiBody);
    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);

    // Force evaluation reads positions only.  Keep the datatype extent equal
    // to Body so body counts/displacements remain directly usable while each
    // timestep exchanges only the position member.
    MPI_Datatype mpiPositionMember;
    MPI_Datatype mpiBodyPositions;
    int positionBlockLength = 3;
    MPI_Aint positionDisplacement = static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos));
    MPI_Type_create_struct(1, &positionBlockLength, &positionDisplacement,
                           memberTypes, &mpiPositionMember);
    MPI_Type_create_resized(mpiPositionMember, 0, static_cast<MPI_Aint>(sizeof(Body)),
                            &mpiBodyPositions);
    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBodyPositions);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiPositionMember);

    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);

    // Scatter the initial state, then make all positions available to every
    // rank.  The root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also
    // its receive buffer for the root-owned range.
    if (numBodies > 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        } else {
            MPI_Scatterv(nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, mpiBody,
                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    
    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
        computeForces(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
        integrateBodies(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);

        // The next timestep needs the newly integrated positions from every
        // rank.  In-place allgather avoids an extra local-to-global copy on
        // the critical path.
        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    }

    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double elapsed = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
    }

    // Velocities are private to their owning rank during the simulation.  A
    // full-state gather is needed only for rank 0's optional result output or
    // validation, and is deliberately outside the timed region.
    if ((printResults || validate) && numBodies > 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
                        bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        } else {
            MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody,
                        nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
        }
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
        std::vector<double> bodyData;
        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
        }
        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
    }

    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
    int exitCode = 0;
    if (validate && rank == 0) {
        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");

        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
            // Report final energy for reference
            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            exitCode = 1;
        }
    }

    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBodyPositions);
    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBody);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return exitCode;
}

+    MPI_Datatype memberTypes[2] = {MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_DOUBLE};
+    MPI_Type_create_struct(2, blockLengths, memberDisplacements, memberTypes, &mpiBody);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);
+
+    // Force evaluation reads positions only.  Keep the datatype extent equal
+    // to Body so body counts/displacements remain directly usable while each
+    // timestep exchanges only the position member.
+    MPI_Datatype mpiPositionMember;
+    MPI_Datatype mpiBodyPositions;
+    int positionBlockLength = 3;
+    MPI_Aint positionDisplacement = static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos));
+    MPI_Type_create_struct(1, &positionBlockLength, &positionDisplacement,
+                           memberTypes, &mpiPositionMember);
+    MPI_Type_create_resized(mpiPositionMember, 0, static_cast<MPI_Aint>(sizeof(Body)),
+                            &mpiBodyPositions);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBodyPositions);
+    MPI_Type_free(&mpiPositionMember);

+    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
+    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
+    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);
+
+    // Scatter the initial state, then make all positions available to every
+    // rank.  The root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also
+    // its receive buffer for the root-owned range.
+    if (numBodies > 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Scatterv(nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, mpiBody,
+                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();

-        computeForces(bodies);
-        integrateBodies(bodies);

+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,

+        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        bodyDisplacements[process] = process == 0
+            ? 0
+            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];
+    }
+
+    std::vector<Body> bodies(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        randomizeBodies(bodies);
+    }
+
+    // Describe the two Vec3 members without assuming that Body is an MPI
+    // primitive type.  Its extent is exactly sizeof(Body), so counts and
+    // displacements below are expressed in bodies rather than bytes.
+    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
+    int blockLengths[2] = {3, 3};
+    MPI_Aint memberDisplacements[2] = {
+        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos)),
+        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, vel))
+    };
+    MPI_Datatype memberTypes[2] = {MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_DOUBLE};
+    MPI_Type_create_struct(2, blockLengths, memberDisplacements, memberTypes, &mpiBody);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);
+
+    // Force evaluation reads positions only.  Keep the datatype extent equal
+    // to Body so body counts/displacements remain directly usable while each
+    // timestep exchanges only the position member.
+    MPI_Datatype mpiPositionMember;
+    MPI_Datatype mpiBodyPositions;
+    int positionBlockLength = 3;
+    MPI_Aint positionDisplacement = static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos));
+    MPI_Type_create_struct(1, &positionBlockLength, &positionDisplacement,
+                           memberTypes, &mpiPositionMember);
+    MPI_Type_create_resized(mpiPositionMember, 0, static_cast<MPI_Aint>(sizeof(Body)),
+                            &mpiBodyPositions);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBodyPositions);
+    MPI_Type_free(&mpiPositionMember);
+
+    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
+    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
+    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);
+
+    // Scatter the initial state, then make all positions available to every
+    // rank.  The root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also
+    // its receive buffer for the root-owned range.
+    if (numBodies > 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
+                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
+                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
     
-    // Run simulation
-    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    
     for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
-        computeForces(bodies);
-        integrateBodies(bodies);
+        computeForces(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
+        integrateBodies(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
+
+        // The next timestep needs the newly integrated positions from every
+        // rank.  In-place allgather avoids an extra local-to-global copy on
+        // the critical path.
+        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
+                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
+                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }
+
+    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double elapsed = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
+        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
+    }
+
+    // Velocities are private to their owning rank during the simulation.  A
+    // full-state gather is needed only for rank 0's optional result output or
+    // validation, and is deliberately outside the timed region.
+    if ((printResults || validate) && numBodies > 0) {
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
+                        bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        } else {
+            MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody,
+                        bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
+                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        }
     }
-

-    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
-    
-    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
-

-    if (printResults) {
+    if (printResults && rank == 0) {

    12	constexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;
    13	constexpr double DT = 0.01;

    15	struct Vec3 {

    17	    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}
    18	};

    28	void randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    29	    for (auto& body : bodies) {
    30	        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    31	        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    32	        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    33	        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    34	        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    35	        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;
    36	    }
    37	}
    38	
    39	void computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const size_t firstBody, const size_t lastBody) {
    40	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    41	
    42	    for (size_t i = firstBody; i < lastBody; ++i) {
    43	        const double posX = bodies[i].pos.x;
    44	        const double posY = bodies[i].pos.y;
    45	        const double posZ = bodies[i].pos.z;
    46	        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;
    47	
    48	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
    49	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - posX;
    50	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - posY;
    51	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - posZ;
    52	            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;
    53	            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);
    54	            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;
    55	
    56	            Fx += dx * invDist3;
    57	            Fy += dy * invDist3;
    58	            Fz += dz * invDist3;
    59	        }
    60	
    61	        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;
    62	        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;
    63	        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;
    64	    }
    65	}
    66	
    67	void integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, const size_t firstBody, const size_t lastBody) {
    68	    for (size_t i = firstBody; i < lastBody; ++i) {
    69	        auto& body = bodies[i];
    70	        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;
    71	        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;
    72	        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;
    73	    }
    74	}
    75	
    76	double computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
    77	    double energy = 0.0;
    78	    const size_t n = bodies.size();
    79	    
    80	    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)
    81	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
    82	        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + 
    83	                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + 
    84	                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);
    85	    }
    86	    
    87	    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)
    88	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
    89	        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
    90	            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;
    91	            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;
    92	            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;
    93	            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);
    94	            energy -= 1.0 / dist;
    95	        }
    96	    }
    97	    
    98	    return energy;
    99	}
   100	
   101	// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   102	bool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {
   103	    for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   104	        // Check for NaN or Inf values
   105	        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||
   106	            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {
   107	            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\n");
   108	            return false;
   109	        }
   110	        
   111	        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)
   112	        const double maxPos = 1e6;
   113	        const double maxVel = 1e6;
   114	        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {
   115	            printf("Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   116	            return false;
   117	        }
   118	        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {
   119	            printf("Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\n");
   120	            return false;
   121	        }
   122	    }
   123	    return true;
   124	}
   125	
   126	void printUsage(const char* progName) {
   127	    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
   128	    printf("Options:\n");
   129	    printf("  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\n");
   130	    printf("  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\n");
   131	    printf("  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\n");
   132	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   133	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   134	}
   135	
   136	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   137	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   138	
   139	    int rank = 0;
   140	    int worldSize = 1;
   141	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   142	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &worldSize);
   143	
   144	    int numBodies = 1024;
   145	    int numSteps = 10;
   146	    bool validate = false;
   147	    bool printResults = false;
   148	    
   149	    // Parse command line arguments
   150	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   151	        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   152	            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);
   153	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   154	            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
   155	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   156	            validate = true;
   157	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   158	            printResults = true;
   159	        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   160	            if (rank == 0) {
   161	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   162	            }
   163	            MPI_Finalize();
   164	            return 0;
   165	        } else {
   166	            if (rank == 0) {
   167	                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   168	                printUsage(argv[0]);
   169	            }
   170	            MPI_Finalize();
   171	            return 1;
   172	        }
   173	    }
   174	
   175	    if (numBodies < 0) {
   176	        if (rank == 0) {
   177	            printf("Number of bodies must be non-negative\n");
   178	        }
   179	        MPI_Finalize();
   180	        return 1;
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    if (rank == 0) {
   184	        printf("N-Body Simulation\n");
   185	        printf("Number of bodies: %d\n", numBodies);
   186	        printf("Number of steps: %d\n", numSteps);
   187	        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   188	    }
   189	
   190	    // Bodies are distributed in contiguous ranges.  Every rank keeps the
   191	    // complete source position state because each force calculation needs
   192	    // every body.
   193	    std::vector<int> bodyCounts(worldSize);
   194	    std::vector<int> bodyDisplacements(worldSize);
   195	    const int bodiesPerRank = numBodies / worldSize;
   196	    const int remainder = numBodies % worldSize;
   197	    for (int process = 0; process < worldSize; ++process) {
   198	        bodyCounts[process] = bodiesPerRank + (process < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   199	        bodyDisplacements[process] = process == 0
   200	            ? 0
   201	            : bodyDisplacements[process - 1] + bodyCounts[process - 1];
   202	    }
   203	
   204	    std::vector<Body> bodies(static_cast<size_t>(numBodies));
   205	    if (rank == 0) {
   206	        randomizeBodies(bodies);
   207	    }
   208	
   209	    // Describe the two Vec3 members without assuming that Body is an MPI
   210	    // primitive type.  Its extent is exactly sizeof(Body), so counts and
   211	    // displacements below are expressed in bodies rather than bytes.
   212	    MPI_Datatype mpiBody;
   213	    int blockLengths[2] = {3, 3};
   214	    MPI_Aint memberDisplacements[2] = {
   215	        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos)),
   216	        static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, vel))
   217	    };
   218	    MPI_Datatype memberTypes[2] = {MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_DOUBLE};
   219	    MPI_Type_create_struct(2, blockLengths, memberDisplacements, memberTypes, &mpiBody);
   220	    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBody);
   221	
   222	    // Force evaluation reads positions only.  Keep the datatype extent equal
   223	    // to Body so body counts/displacements remain directly usable while each
   224	    // timestep exchanges only the position member.
   225	    MPI_Datatype mpiPositionMember;
   226	    MPI_Datatype mpiBodyPositions;
   227	    int positionBlockLength = 3;
   228	    MPI_Aint positionDisplacement = static_cast<MPI_Aint>(offsetof(Body, pos));
   229	    MPI_Type_create_struct(1, &positionBlockLength, &positionDisplacement,
   230	                           memberTypes, &mpiPositionMember);
   231	    MPI_Type_create_resized(mpiPositionMember, 0, static_cast<MPI_Aint>(sizeof(Body)),
   232	                            &mpiBodyPositions);
   233	    MPI_Type_commit(&mpiBodyPositions);
   234	    MPI_Type_free(&mpiPositionMember);
   235	
   236	    const int localCount = bodyCounts[rank];
   237	    const size_t firstBody = static_cast<size_t>(bodyDisplacements[rank]);
   238	    const size_t lastBody = firstBody + static_cast<size_t>(localCount);
   239	
   240	    // Scatter the initial state, then make all positions available to every
   241	    // rank.  The root uses MPI_IN_PLACE because its initialized array is also
   242	    // its receive buffer for the root-owned range.
   243	    if (numBodies > 0) {
   244	        if (rank == 0) {
   245	            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
   246	                         MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   247	        } else {
   248	            MPI_Scatterv(bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
   249	                         bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   250	        }
   251	        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
   252	                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
   253	                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   254	    }
   255	
   256	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   257	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   258	    
   259	    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {
   260	        computeForces(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
   261	        integrateBodies(bodies, firstBody, lastBody);
   262	
   263	        // The next timestep needs the newly integrated positions from every
   264	        // rank.  In-place allgather avoids an extra local-to-global copy on
   265	        // the critical path.
   266	        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBodyPositions,
   267	                       bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBodyPositions,
   268	                       MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   269	    }
   270	
   271	    const double localElapsed = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   272	    double elapsed = 0.0;
   273	    MPI_Reduce(&localElapsed, &elapsed, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   274	
   275	    if (rank == 0) {
   276	        const long durationMilliseconds = static_cast<long>(elapsed * 1000.0);
   277	        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", durationMilliseconds);
   278	    }
   279	
   280	    // Velocities are private to their owning rank during the simulation.  A
   281	    // full-state gather is needed only for rank 0's optional result output or
   282	    // validation, and is deliberately outside the timed region.
   283	    if ((printResults || validate) && numBodies > 0) {
   284	        if (rank == 0) {
   285	            MPI_Gatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, mpiBody,
   286	                        bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
   287	                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   288	        } else {
   289	            MPI_Gatherv(bodies.data() + firstBody, localCount, mpiBody,
   290	                        bodies.data(), bodyCounts.data(), bodyDisplacements.data(), mpiBody,
   291	                        0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   292	        }
   293	    }
   294	
   295	    // Print results for external validation
   296	    if (printResults && rank == 0) {
   297	        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing
   298	        std::vector<double> bodyData;
   299	        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);
   300	        for (const auto& body : bodies) {
   301	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);
   302	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);
   303	            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);
   304	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);
   305	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);
   306	            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);
   307	        }
   308	        print_results(bodyData, "Bodies");
   309	    }
   310	
   311	    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values
   312	    int exitCode = 0;
   313	    if (validate && rank == 0) {
   314	        printf("Validating simulation results...\n");
   315	
   316	        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {
   317	            // Report final energy for reference
   318	            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);
   319	            printf("Final energy: %.6f\n", finalEnergy);
   320	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   321	        } else {
   322	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   323	            exitCode = 1;
   324	        }
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   328	    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBodyPositions);
   329	    MPI_Type_free(&mpiBody);
   330	    MPI_Finalize();
